## Best date plans

### 2026-10-08 — Rooftop Films Screening: Practical Magic + Good Time Country Buffet

**Rooftop Films Screening: Practical Magic, then dinner.** Southern buffet at Good Time Country Buffet. It is 1.7 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 6 pm, so eat afterwards.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=25+11th+Ave%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10011&destination=111+E+7th+St%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10009&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://pier57nyc.com/event/rooftop-films-screening-practical-magic/)

### 2026-10-07 — Le Rock + Central Park Conservancy Film Festival: Time and Water

**Dinner, then Central Park Conservancy Film Festival: Time and Water.** French brasserie at Le Rock. It is a 20-minute walk from the venue. Doors 5:30 pm — eat first.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=45+Rockefeller+Plaza%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10111&destination=Enter+near+W+69th+St%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10023&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.centralparknyc.org/activities/guides/central-park-conservancy-film-festival) · [Restaurant](https://www.lerocknyc.com/) · [Reservations](https://www.lerocknyc.com/reservations)

## What to do this week

### 2026-10-07

#### [Central Park Conservancy Film Festival: Time and Water](https://www.centralparknyc.org/activities/guides/central-park-conservancy-film-festival)

7:00 PM · outdoors · Free · Central Park lawn between Cherry Hill and Sheep Meadow · Central Park · Manhattan · Score 75.0  
The Central Park Conservancy's 23rd annual outdoor film festival, presented with National Geographic, continues with a free dusk screening in the park.
**Timing:** Gates open 5:30pm; film starts at dusk, around 7pm.
**Practical note:** NWS forecast is mostly clear Wednesday night; bring a layer and ground seating.
**Why now:** The festival runs Tuesday through Friday this week, weather permitting.
[Official](https://www.centralparknyc.org/activities/guides/central-park-conservancy-film-festival) · [Discovered via The Skint](https://theskint.com/)

#### [Festival of Firsts](https://www.lincolncenter.org/series/lincoln-center-presents/s/Festival%20of%20Firsts)

6:00 PM · theater · Free · David Rubenstein Atrium at Lincoln Center · Upper West Side · Manhattan · Score 70.0  
Lincoln Center's fifth Festival of Firsts presents two weeks of premieres and collaborations across theater, music, dance, and interdisciplinary performance.
**Timing:** Festival runs October 7-23; confirm the specific daily performance time on Lincoln Center's calendar.
**Practical note:** Free indoor Atrium programming; arrive early for limited seating.
**Why now:** Opening day of a two-week festival in the requested window.
**On-sale watch:** Free Lincoln Center programs can fill before showtime.

#### [MoMA free Friday evening](https://www.moma.org/visit/)

4:00 PM · museums · Free · Museum of Modern Art · Midtown · Manhattan · Score 46.7  
MoMA offers free admission on Friday evenings. Free tickets are limited, released in advance, and go quickly.
**Timing:** Free Friday evenings, roughly 4-8pm. Confirm the current window and ticket release on moma.org.
**Practical note:** Book the free timed ticket the moment it is released; the line for walk-ups is long.
**Why now:** A standing weekly free window at a museum that is otherwise one of the most expensive in the city.
[Official](https://www.moma.org/visit/)

#### [Prospect Park Carousel](https://www.prospectpark.org/visit-the-park/places-to-go/carousel/)

12:00 PM · outdoors · $3/ride; $13 book of 5 · Prospect Park Carousel · Prospect Park · Brooklyn · Score 49.0  
Prospect Park's restored 1912 Charles Carmel carousel runs with 53 horses, a lion, a giraffe, a deer, and two dragon-pulled chariots.
**Timing:** Seasonal hours noon-5pm Thursday-Sunday and holidays, per listing.
**Practical note:** Good low-cost add-on after a park walk; Saturday weather is favorable.
**Why now:** The carousel's seasonal public hours continue through November 23.

## Where to eat

Checked the risky dining claims against captured public evidence and limited follow-up public pages; kept useful current offers/events while downgrading ordinary menus and rejecting same-restaurant duplicates.

Dining research: 2026-10-06T10:30:46+00:00

No Michelin star claims were retained. The Kirbee's source mentions Michelin-related recognition for the operators' Texas restaurants, not an award for Kirbee's; that distinction should remain clear. Access limits: Motek happy-hour PDF text was unreadable, the Queens Farm TicketSpice page exposed only purchase-protection text, and the CB3 PDF was blocked.

### Good Time Country Buffet all-you-can-eat dinner

Deals & happy hours — Good Time Country Buffet

New buffet from the Kisa and C as in Charlie team with fried chicken, collards, mac and cheese, rotating dishes, salad bar, and unlimited trips through the line; useful as a comparatively affordable sit-down dinner.

Price: $26.99 adults; $23.99 seniors 65+; $13.99 kids ages 4-9; desserts and boozy frozen drinks extra  
Schedule: Wednesday-Sunday, 5:00 PM-10:00 PM  
Conditions: Walk-ins plus a limited number of Resy reservations; sweet tea/soda, desserts, beer, and frozen drinks cost extra where stated; tax/tip/service charges not stated.  
From: 2026-09-12  
Opened: 2026-09-12  
Published: 2026-09-10  
[Source](https://ny.eater.com/news/413774/good-time-country-buffet-east-village-opening-kisa)

### Fish Cheeks NoHo happy hour window

Deals & happy hours — Fish Cheeks

Official location metadata lists a daily happy-hour window at the original NoHo Thai seafood spot; exact happy-hour item pricing was not exposed in accessible text, so treat it as a timing lead to verify before going.

Schedule: Happy hour 12:00 PM-5:00 PM; restaurant hours Sunday-Thursday 12:00 PM-10:15 PM, Friday-Saturday 12:00 PM-11:15 PM  
Conditions: Official Fish Cheeks structured location data confirms the NoHo happy-hour window but not item prices or exclusions; verify current happy-hour items day-of. Source: https://www.fishcheeksnyc.com/menus/  
[Source](https://www.fishcheeksnyc.com/menus/)

### Fish Cheeks Williamsburg happy hour window

Deals & happy hours — Fish Cheeks Williamsburg

Brooklyn branch has a longer official happy-hour window than NoHo, making it a useful Williamsburg Thai-seafood drinks/snacks target if the current item list checks out day-of.

Schedule: Happy hour every day 12:00 PM-6:00 PM; restaurant hours Sunday-Thursday 12:00 PM-10:15 PM, Friday-Saturday 12:00 PM-10:45 PM  
Conditions: Official Fish Cheeks structured location data confirms the Williamsburg happy-hour window but not item prices or exclusions; verify current happy-hour items day-of. Source: https://www.fishcheeksnyc.com/menus/  
[Source](https://www.fishcheeksnyc.com/menus/)

### Ci Siamo weekday aperitivo menu in the bar and lounge

Special menus — Ci Siamo

Official aperitivo menu offers a bounded weekday late-afternoon bar/lounge option with snacks such as mortadella, pizza bianca, pizzette, smashed potatoes, and focaccia.
